{"parentUuid":"32b085c6-ffd7-4df6-945e-f63221ae25c7","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01JvxSixS1XKF6iURmFWaSEJ","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Pure geometry for the Trace Map \"Leitstand\" radial SVG map\n4\t  (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand, AC2).\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n7\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n8\t  (the interactive dummy `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`'s own `MODEL`/`R1`/\n9\t  `R2`/`rad()`/`edge()` code is the numeric ground truth this module mirrors).\n10\t\n11\t  A plain Elixir module — NO LiveView/HTML knowledge (spec §8 constraint 3,\n12\t  ADR-018/019/021): `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.render/1` renders the SVG\n13\t  server-side in HEEx from the `{x, y}` positions computed here; LiveView\n14\t  patches only ever update node classes/counters, never positions.\n15\t\n16\t  All constants use the spec's viewBox `-560 -400 1120 800` coordinate space:\n17\t\n18\t    * nebu (center) circle r=52, always at the origin `{0.0, 0.0}`\n19\t    * domain circles r=38 on the R1=195 ring around nebu\n20\t    * satellite circles r=24 on the R2=128 ring around THEIR OWN domain\n21\t      (not around nebu), fanned over `min(130°, n·26°)` centered on the\n22\t      domain's outward angle\n23\t    * edges are straight lines trimmed by (node radius + 4px) at each end\n24\t  \"\"\"\n25\t\n26\t  @center_radius 52\n27\t  @domain_radius 38\n28\t  @satellite_radius 24\n29\t  @domain_ring 195.0\n30\t  @satellite_ring 128.0\n31\t  @edge_padding 4\n32\t\n33\t  # Spec §2's literal, deliberately hand-placed (NOT evenly spaced) layout for\n34\t  # the canonical 4 named domains, in this exact order. Any other key list —\n35\t  # different order, different count, different names — falls back to the even\n36\t  # distribution below.\n37\t  @canonical_domains [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\"]\n38\t  @canonical_angles [-48.0, 48.0, 132.0, -132.0]\n39\t\n40\t  @doc \"\"\"\n41\t  One `{domain_key, angle_degrees}` pair per input key, in input order\n42\t  (first-observed order, never re-sorted).\n43\t\n44\t  The exact canonical 4-domain list (spec order) gets the spec's fixed angles\n45\t  `[-48, 48, 132, -132]`; anything else is distributed evenly starting at\n46\t  -90°, stepping `360 / n`.\n47\t  \"\"\"\n48\t  @spec domain_angles([String.t()]) :: [{String.t(), float()}]\n49\t  def domain_angles([]), do: []\n50\t\n51\t  def domain_angles(@canonical_domains = keys), do: Enum.zip(keys, @canonical_angles)\n52\t\n53\t  def domain_angles(keys) when is_list(keys) do\n54\t    step = 360.0 / length(keys)\n55\t\n56\t    keys\n57\t    |> Enum.with_index()\n58\t    |> Enum.map(fn {key, i} -> {key, -90.0 + i * step} end)\n59\t  end\n60\t\n61\t  @doc \"\"\"\n62\t  `count` satellite angles fanned around `domain_angle`, spread over\n63\t  `min(130°, count·26°)` (spec §2). A single satellite sits exactly on the\n64\t  domain's own angle (no spread).\n65\t  \"\"\"\n66\t  @spec satellite_angles(float(), pos_integer()) :: [float()]\n67\t  def satellite_angles(domain_angle, 1), do: [domain_angle]\n68\t\n69\t  def satellite_angles(domain_angle, count) when is_integer(count) and count > 1 do\n70\t    spread = min(130.0, count * 26.0)\n71\t    step = spread / (count - 1)\n72\t\n73\t    for i <- 0..(count - 1), do: domain_angle - spread / 2 + i * step\n74\t  end\n75\t\n76\t  @doc \"\"\"\n77\t  Standard SVG polar-to-cartesian conversion (positive Y is down; no Y-flip —\n78\t  same convention as the dummy's own `Math.cos`/`Math.sin` usage).\n79\t  \"\"\"\n80\t  @spec polar_to_cartesian(number(), number()) :: {float(), float()}\n81\t  def polar_to_cartesian(angle_degrees, radius) do\n82\t    radians = angle_degrees * :math.pi() / 180.0\n83\t    {radius * :math.cos(radians), radius * :math.sin(radians)}\n84\t  end\n85\t\n86\t  @doc \"\"\"\n87\t  `{x1, y1, x2, y2}` for a straight edge from node `a` to node `b`, shortened\n88\t  at each end by that end's own `radius + 4px` (spec §2 \"node edge + 4px\";\n89\t  mirrors the dummy's `edge()` function exactly).\n90\t  \"\"\"\n91\t  @spec trim_edge(%{x: number(), y: number(), r: number()}, %{\n92\t          x: number(),\n93\t          y: number(),\n94\t          r: number()\n95\t        }) :: {float(), float(), float(), float()}\n96\t  def trim_edge(a, b) do\n97\t    dx = b.x - a.x\n98\t    dy = b.y - a.y\n99\t    len = :math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)\n100\t    ux = dx / len\n101\t    uy = dy / len\n102\t\n103\t    {\n104\t      a.x + ux * (a.r + @edge_padding),\n105\t      a.y + uy * (a.r + @edge_padding),\n106\t      b.x - ux * (b.r + @edge_padding),\n107\t      b.y - uy * (b.r + @edge_padding)\n108\t    }\n109\t  end\n110\t\n111\t  @doc \"\"\"\n112\t  The full computed radial layout for `domains` (a list of\n113\t  `%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}`, first-observed order):\n114\t\n115\t    * `nebu` — always `%{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}`.\n116\t    * `domains` — `%{domain_key => %{x, y, r, angle}}`, on the R1=195 ring.\n117\t    * `satellites` — `%{satellite_key => %{x, y, r, angle, domain}}`; the\n118\t      absolute position is the PARENT DOMAIN's position plus the satellite's\n119\t      own polar offset on the R2=128 ring.\n120\t  \"\"\"\n121\t  @spec layout([%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}]) :: %{\n122\t          nebu: map(),\n123\t          domains: %{String.t() => map()},\n124\t          satellites: %{String.t() => map()}\n125\t        }\n126\t  def layout(domains) do\n127\t    angle_by_key = domains |> Enum.map(& &1.key) |> domain_angles() |> Map.new()\n128\t\n129\t    domain_map =\n130\t      Map.new(domains, fn %{key: key} ->\n131\t        angle = Map.fetch!(angle_by_key, key)\n132\t        {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, @domain_ring)\n133\t        {key, %{x: x, y: y, r: @domain_radius, angle: angle}}\n134\t      end)\n135\t\n136\t    satellite_map =\n137\t      domains\n138\t      |> Enum.flat_map(fn %{key: domain_key, satellites: satellites} ->\n139\t        domain = Map.fetch!(domain_map, domain_key)\n140\t        angles = if satellites == [], do: [], else: satellite_angles(domain.angle, length(satellites))\n141\t\n142\t        satellites\n143\t        |> Enum.zip(angles)\n144\t        |> Enum.map(fn {satellite_key, angle} ->\n145\t          {dx, dy} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, @satellite_ring)\n146\t\n147\t          {satellite_key,\n148\t           %{\n149\t             x: domain.x + dx,\n150\t             y: domain.y + dy,\n151\t             r: @satellite_radius,\n152\t             angle: angle,\n153\t             domain: domain_key\n154\t           }}\n155\t        end)\n156\t      end)\n157\t      |> Map.new()\n158\t\n159\t    %{\n160\t      nebu: %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: @center_radius},\n161\t      domains: domain_map,\n162\t      satellites: satellite_map\n163\t    }\n164\t  end\n165\tend\n166\t"}]},"uuid":"9e02cdab-2c8f-4026-8488-b28a9e5676f7","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:35:47.273Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/trace_map_geometry.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Pure geometry for the Trace Map \"Leitstand\" radial SVG map\n  (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand, AC2).\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n  (the interactive dummy `trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html`'s own `MODEL`/`R1`/\n  `R2`/`rad()`/`edge()` code is the numeric ground truth this module mirrors).\n\n  A plain Elixir module — NO LiveView/HTML knowledge (spec §8 constraint 3,\n  ADR-018/019/021): `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap.render/1` renders the SVG\n  server-side in HEEx from the `{x, y}` positions computed here; LiveView\n  patches only ever update node classes/counters, never positions.\n\n  All constants use the spec's viewBox `-560 -400 1120 800` coordinate space:\n\n    * nebu (center) circle r=52, always at the origin `{0.0, 0.0}`\n    * domain circles r=38 on the R1=195 ring around nebu\n    * satellite circles r=24 on the R2=128 ring around THEIR OWN domain\n      (not around nebu), fanned over `min(130°, n·26°)` centered on the\n      domain's outward angle\n    * edges are straight lines trimmed by (node radius + 4px) at each end\n  \"\"\"\n\n  @center_radius 52\n  @domain_radius 38\n  @satellite_radius 24\n  @domain_ring 195.0\n  @satellite_ring 128.0\n  @edge_padding 4\n\n  # Spec §2's literal, deliberately hand-placed (NOT evenly spaced) layout for\n  # the canonical 4 named domains, in this exact order. Any other key list —\n  # different order, different count, different names — falls back to the even\n  # distribution below.\n  @canonical_domains [\"/_matrix/client\", \"/_matrix/media\", \"/admin\", \"/health\"]\n  @canonical_angles [-48.0, 48.0, 132.0, -132.0]\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  One `{domain_key, angle_degrees}` pair per input key, in input order\n  (first-observed order, never re-sorted).\n\n  The exact canonical 4-domain list (spec order) gets the spec's fixed angles\n  `[-48, 48, 132, -132]`; anything else is distributed evenly starting at\n  -90°, stepping `360 / n`.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec domain_angles([String.t()]) :: [{String.t(), float()}]\n  def domain_angles([]), do: []\n\n  def domain_angles(@canonical_domains = keys), do: Enum.zip(keys, @canonical_angles)\n\n  def domain_angles(keys) when is_list(keys) do\n    step = 360.0 / length(keys)\n\n    keys\n    |> Enum.with_index()\n    |> Enum.map(fn {key, i} -> {key, -90.0 + i * step} end)\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  `count` satellite angles fanned around `domain_angle`, spread over\n  `min(130°, count·26°)` (spec §2). A single satellite sits exactly on the\n  domain's own angle (no spread).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec satellite_angles(float(), pos_integer()) :: [float()]\n  def satellite_angles(domain_angle, 1), do: [domain_angle]\n\n  def satellite_angles(domain_angle, count) when is_integer(count) and count > 1 do\n    spread = min(130.0, count * 26.0)\n    step = spread / (count - 1)\n\n    for i <- 0..(count - 1), do: domain_angle - spread / 2 + i * step\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Standard SVG polar-to-cartesian conversion (positive Y is down; no Y-flip —\n  same convention as the dummy's own `Math.cos`/`Math.sin` usage).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec polar_to_cartesian(number(), number()) :: {float(), float()}\n  def polar_to_cartesian(angle_degrees, radius) do\n    radians = angle_degrees * :math.pi() / 180.0\n    {radius * :math.cos(radians), radius * :math.sin(radians)}\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  `{x1, y1, x2, y2}` for a straight edge from node `a` to node `b`, shortened\n  at each end by that end's own `radius + 4px` (spec §2 \"node edge + 4px\";\n  mirrors the dummy's `edge()` function exactly).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec trim_edge(%{x: number(), y: number(), r: number()}, %{\n          x: number(),\n          y: number(),\n          r: number()\n        }) :: {float(), float(), float(), float()}\n  def trim_edge(a, b) do\n    dx = b.x - a.x\n    dy = b.y - a.y\n    len = :math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy)\n    ux = dx / len\n    uy = dy / len\n\n    {\n      a.x + ux * (a.r + @edge_padding),\n      a.y + uy * (a.r + @edge_padding),\n      b.x - ux * (b.r + @edge_padding),\n      b.y - uy * (b.r + @edge_padding)\n    }\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  The full computed radial layout for `domains` (a list of\n  `%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}`, first-observed order):\n\n    * `nebu` — always `%{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: 52}`.\n    * `domains` — `%{domain_key => %{x, y, r, angle}}`, on the R1=195 ring.\n    * `satellites` — `%{satellite_key => %{x, y, r, angle, domain}}`; the\n      absolute position is the PARENT DOMAIN's position plus the satellite's\n      own polar offset on the R2=128 ring.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec layout([%{key: String.t(), satellites: [String.t()]}]) :: %{\n          nebu: map(),\n          domains: %{String.t() => map()},\n          satellites: %{String.t() => map()}\n        }\n  def layout(domains) do\n    angle_by_key = domains |> Enum.map(& &1.key) |> domain_angles() |> Map.new()\n\n    domain_map =\n      Map.new(domains, fn %{key: key} ->\n        angle = Map.fetch!(angle_by_key, key)\n        {x, y} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, @domain_ring)\n        {key, %{x: x, y: y, r: @domain_radius, angle: angle}}\n      end)\n\n    satellite_map =\n      domains\n      |> Enum.flat_map(fn %{key: domain_key, satellites: satellites} ->\n        domain = Map.fetch!(domain_map, domain_key)\n        angles = if satellites == [], do: [], else: satellite_angles(domain.angle, length(satellites))\n\n        satellites\n        |> Enum.zip(angles)\n        |> Enum.map(fn {satellite_key, angle} ->\n          {dx, dy} = polar_to_cartesian(angle, @satellite_ring)\n\n          {satellite_key,\n           %{\n             x: domain.x + dx,\n             y: domain.y + dy,\n             r: @satellite_radius,\n             angle: angle,\n             domain: domain_key\n           }}\n        end)\n      end)\n      |> Map.new()\n\n    %{\n      nebu: %{x: 0.0, y: 0.0, r: @center_radius},\n      domains: domain_map,\n      satellites: satellite_map\n    }\n  end\nend\n","numLines":166,"startLine":1,"totalLines":166}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"32b085c6-ffd7-4df6-945e-f63221ae25c7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"9e02cdab-2c8f-4026-8488-b28a9e5676f7","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cco6peaUifJ9Bdva5nsE2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01S6STjf12tdqQjUmgKcfbyz","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":25894,"cache_read_input_tokens":16750,"output_tokens":264,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":25894},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":264,"cache_read_input_tokens":16750,"cache_creation_input_tokens":25894,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":25894,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cco6pdJaX6SzW3BxFckGg","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"10b9b35d-884b-40ab-a3c2-7eb415dbc90e","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:35:47.964Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"10b9b35d-884b-40ab-a3c2-7eb415dbc90e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01S6STjf12tdqQjUmgKcfbyz","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Per-node status model for the Trace Map \"Leitstand\"\n4\t  (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand, AC3): a 60-second sliding window\n5\t  over a node's log events, with worst-child propagation and automatic\n6\t  healing.\n7\t\n8\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n9\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n10\t\n11\t  A plain, pure module (ADR-018/021): `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` keeps\n12\t  one state per map node (nebu, each domain, each satellite) in a socket\n13\t  assign and drives every timestamp with plain integer milliseconds\n14\t  (`System.system_time(:millisecond)` at ingest; `inserted_at`-derived for\n15\t  mount-time history) — deterministic and directly unit-testable with exact\n16\t  integers, no `DateTime` arithmetic.\n17\t\n18\t  ## The window is a pure function of `(state, now_ms)` — auto-heal falls out\n19\t\n20\t  `counts/2`/`status/2` never mutate state: every call independently filters\n21\t  the recorded events by `now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`. There is\n22\t  deliberately NO `heal/1`/`ack/1` operation (spec §2: \"Nodes heal\n23\t  automatically when the window passes — no manual acknowledge\") — the SAME\n24\t  state, queried at a later `now_ms` once every event has aged out, is `:ok`\n25\t  with zero counts, with no code path that could \"forget\" to re-evaluate it.\n26\t\n27\t  Note the deliberate departure from the sibling `/admin/logs` page's Trace\n28\t  Map bubbles: THOSE counts are cumulative since page load; here ALL three\n29\t  counts — including requests — are windowed (AC3's literal text).\n30\t\n31\t  ## Memory bound\n32\t\n33\t  `record/3` prunes events that had already aged out of the window ending at\n34\t  the newly recorded timestamp, so a long-lived LiveView holding many node\n35\t  states stays bounded by 60 seconds of traffic per node, with no separate\n36\t  cleanup timer needed.\n37\t  \"\"\"\n38\t\n39\t  @window_ms 60_000\n40\t\n41\t  @type kind :: :request | :warning | :error\n42\t  @type t :: %{requests: [integer()], warnings: [integer()], errors: [integer()]}\n43\t\n44\t  @rank %{ok: 0, warn: 1, error: 2}\n45\t\n46\t  @doc \"An empty node state (no events recorded yet).\"\n47\t  @spec new() :: t()\n48\t  def new, do: %{requests: [], warnings: [], errors: []}\n49\t\n50\t  @doc \"\"\"\n51\t  Appends one event of `kind` at `timestamp_ms`, pruning events that have\n52\t  already aged out of the window ending at `timestamp_ms` (see moduledoc).\n53\t  \"\"\"\n54\t  @spec record(t(), kind(), integer()) :: t()\n55\t  def record(state, kind, timestamp_ms) do\n56\t    key = key_for(kind)\n57\t    Map.update!(state, key, fn events -> [timestamp_ms | prune(events, timestamp_ms)] end)\n58\t  end\n59\t\n60\t  @doc \"\"\"\n61\t  Counts of events of each kind still inside the 60-second window ending at\n62\t  `now_ms` (`now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`). Pure — never mutates state.\n63\t  \"\"\"\n64\t  @spec counts(t(), integer()) :: %{\n65\t          requests: non_neg_integer(),\n66\t          warnings: non_neg_integer(),\n67\t          errors: non_neg_integer()\n68\t        }\n69\t  def counts(state, now_ms) do\n70\t    %{\n71\t      requests: count_in_window(state.requests, now_ms),\n72\t      warnings: count_in_window(state.warnings, now_ms),\n73\t      errors: count_in_window(state.errors, now_ms)\n74\t    }\n75\t  end\n76\t\n77\t  @doc \"\"\"\n78\t  Spec §2's literal precedence rule at `now_ms`: `errors > 0` -> `:error`,\n79\t  else `warnings > 0` -> `:warn`, else `:ok`.\n80\t  \"\"\"\n81\t  @spec status(t(), integer()) :: :ok | :warn | :error\n82\t  def status(state, now_ms) do\n83\t    cond do\n84\t      count_in_window(state.errors, now_ms) > 0 -> :error\n85\t      count_in_window(state.warnings, now_ms) > 0 -> :warn\n86\t      true -> :ok\n87\t    end\n88\t  end\n89\t\n90\t  @doc \"\"\"\n91\t  Worst-child propagation (spec §2: a parent's status is the worst of its\n92\t  children): `:error` > `:warn` > `:ok`. An empty list is `:ok`.\n93\t  \"\"\"\n94\t  @spec worst([:ok | :warn | :error]) :: :ok | :warn | :error\n95\t  def worst([]), do: :ok\n96\t  def worst(statuses), do: Enum.max_by(statuses, &Map.fetch!(@rank, &1))\n97\t\n98\t  defp key_for(:request), do: :requests\n99\t  defp key_for(:warning), do: :warnings\n100\t  defp key_for(:error), do: :errors\n101\t\n102\t  defp count_in_window(events, now_ms), do: Enum.count(events, &(now_ms - &1 < @window_ms))\n103\t\n104\t  defp prune(events, now_ms), do: Enum.filter(events, &(now_ms - &1 < @window_ms))\n105\tend\n106\t"}]},"uuid":"c51ae578-7430-491d-83ec-26431f5ab4c4","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:35:48.008Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex","content":"defmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus do\n  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n  Per-node status model for the Trace Map \"Leitstand\"\n  (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand, AC3): a 60-second sliding window\n  over a node's log events, with worst-child propagation and automatic\n  healing.\n\n  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md §2\n\n  A plain, pure module (ADR-018/021): `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap` keeps\n  one state per map node (nebu, each domain, each satellite) in a socket\n  assign and drives every timestamp with plain integer milliseconds\n  (`System.system_time(:millisecond)` at ingest; `inserted_at`-derived for\n  mount-time history) — deterministic and directly unit-testable with exact\n  integers, no `DateTime` arithmetic.\n\n  ## The window is a pure function of `(state, now_ms)` — auto-heal falls out\n\n  `counts/2`/`status/2` never mutate state: every call independently filters\n  the recorded events by `now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`. There is\n  deliberately NO `heal/1`/`ack/1` operation (spec §2: \"Nodes heal\n  automatically when the window passes — no manual acknowledge\") — the SAME\n  state, queried at a later `now_ms` once every event has aged out, is `:ok`\n  with zero counts, with no code path that could \"forget\" to re-evaluate it.\n\n  Note the deliberate departure from the sibling `/admin/logs` page's Trace\n  Map bubbles: THOSE counts are cumulative since page load; here ALL three\n  counts — including requests — are windowed (AC3's literal text).\n\n  ## Memory bound\n\n  `record/3` prunes events that had already aged out of the window ending at\n  the newly recorded timestamp, so a long-lived LiveView holding many node\n  states stays bounded by 60 seconds of traffic per node, with no separate\n  cleanup timer needed.\n  \"\"\"\n\n  @window_ms 60_000\n\n  @type kind :: :request | :warning | :error\n  @type t :: %{requests: [integer()], warnings: [integer()], errors: [integer()]}\n\n  @rank %{ok: 0, warn: 1, error: 2}\n\n  @doc \"An empty node state (no events recorded yet).\"\n  @spec new() :: t()\n  def new, do: %{requests: [], warnings: [], errors: []}\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Appends one event of `kind` at `timestamp_ms`, pruning events that have\n  already aged out of the window ending at `timestamp_ms` (see moduledoc).\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec record(t(), kind(), integer()) :: t()\n  def record(state, kind, timestamp_ms) do\n    key = key_for(kind)\n    Map.update!(state, key, fn events -> [timestamp_ms | prune(events, timestamp_ms)] end)\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Counts of events of each kind still inside the 60-second window ending at\n  `now_ms` (`now_ms - timestamp_ms < 60_000`). Pure — never mutates state.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec counts(t(), integer()) :: %{\n          requests: non_neg_integer(),\n          warnings: non_neg_integer(),\n          errors: non_neg_integer()\n        }\n  def counts(state, now_ms) do\n    %{\n      requests: count_in_window(state.requests, now_ms),\n      warnings: count_in_window(state.warnings, now_ms),\n      errors: count_in_window(state.errors, now_ms)\n    }\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Spec §2's literal precedence rule at `now_ms`: `errors > 0` -> `:error`,\n  else `warnings > 0` -> `:warn`, else `:ok`.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec status(t(), integer()) :: :ok | :warn | :error\n  def status(state, now_ms) do\n    cond do\n      count_in_window(state.errors, now_ms) > 0 -> :error\n      count_in_window(state.warnings, now_ms) > 0 -> :warn\n      true -> :ok\n    end\n  end\n\n  @doc \"\"\"\n  Worst-child propagation (spec §2: a parent's status is the worst of its\n  children): `:error` > `:warn` > `:ok`. An empty list is `:ok`.\n  \"\"\"\n  @spec worst([:ok | :warn | :error]) :: :ok | :warn | :error\n  def worst([]), do: :ok\n  def worst(statuses), do: Enum.max_by(statuses, &Map.fetch!(@rank, &1))\n\n  defp key_for(:request), do: :requests\n  defp key_for(:warning), do: :warnings\n  defp key_for(:error), do: :errors\n\n  defp count_in_window(events, now_ms), do: Enum.count(events, &(now_ms - &1 < @window_ms))\n\n  defp prune(events, now_ms), do: Enum.filter(events, &(now_ms - &1 < @window_ms))\nend\n","numLines":106,"startLine":1,"totalLines":106}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"10b9b35d-884b-40ab-a3c2-7eb415dbc90e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"4630dccd-a860-459b-8a55-f63713ca4f8d","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cco6q6f84nCZ658qgVwQk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01WUntgAbu3Zpu7Ayz2bExq2","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5272,"cache_read_input_tokens":42644,"output_tokens":294,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5272},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":294,"cache_read_input_tokens":42644,"cache_creation_input_tokens":5272,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":5272,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cco6q3zdjWSY5ma9EATSc","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"2c64f658-9625-47b6-879f-e1e7da2d9c6a","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:35:56.255Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"2c64f658-9625-47b6-879f-e1e7da2d9c6a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01WUntgAbu3Zpu7Ayz2bExq2","type":"tool_result","content":"1\tdefmodule Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMap do\n2\t  @moduledoc \"\"\"\n3\t  Trace Map \"Leitstand\" — the dedicated, full-page radial health map at\n4\t  `/admin/logs/trace-map` (enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand).\n5\t\n6\t  Story: docs/stories/enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand.md\n7\t  Design: _bmad-output/nebu/epics/enhanced-logging/design/trace-map-leitstand-spec.md\n8\t  Visual reference: trace-map-leitstand-dummy.html (same directory).\n9\t\n10\t  ## Why this is a SEPARATE LiveView module, not a new action on `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`\n11\t\n12\t  AC5 hard-requires URL-driven filter state via `push_patch/2` +\n13\t  `handle_params/3` (the lesson of trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix: filter\n14\t  state must live in LiveView assigns seeded from the URL, NEVER in\n15\t  client-DOM-only state). Merely EXPORTING a `handle_params/3` callback on a\n16\t  LiveView module makes every `live_isolated/3`-mounted test of that module\n17\t  fail at mount (empirically verified against the installed\n18\t  `phoenix_live_view` — see `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s own moduledoc). The\n19\t  existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` (Live Stream page) has a large,\n20\t  deliberately-`live_isolated/3`-based suite, so the Leitstand gets its own\n21\t  module and `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` stays `handle_params/3`-free.\n22\t\n23\t  ## State model\n24\t\n25\t    * `@selected` — the active endpoint filters (satellite routes and/or\n26\t      domain keys), URL-driven: `handle_params/3` seeds it from the\n27\t      bracket-array `?endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=Y` query params; every node\n28\t      click round-trips through `handle_event(\"toggle_node\"/\"clear_filters\")`\n29\t      -> `push_patch/2` -> `handle_params/3`. Bracket-array (not the story's\n30\t      literal repeated bare `?endpoint=X&endpoint=Y`) per the story's own\n31\t      \"Story Amendments\": `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1` is last-wins on repeated\n32\t      bare keys, so the bare form cannot round-trip.\n33\t    * `@node_states` — one `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus` state per map\n34\t      node (`\"nebu\"`, each domain key, each satellite route): the AC3\n35\t      60-second sliding window. Events are recorded at ALL THREE levels on\n36\t      ingest so every node carries its own windowed request/warning/error\n37\t      counts; a parent's STATUS is still derived via\n38\t      `TraceMapStatus.worst/1` over its children (worst-child propagation,\n39\t      spec §2), with the parent's own aggregated state included defensively\n40\t      (covers nil-route entries that belong to no domain, for nebu).\n41\t    * `@active_levels` — the stream panel's level filter (AC9), plain\n42\t      assigns state (the leitstand shortcut is computed server-side in\n43\t      `handle_event(\"toggle_level\", ...)`).\n44\t    * Geometry is computed server-side by `Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry`\n45\t      (spec §8 constraint 3); domain grouping REUSES\n46\t      `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1` via\n47\t      `group_endpoints_by_domain/1` (never duplicated).\n48\t\n49\t  ## PubSub / data source\n50\t\n51\t  Same source as the Live Stream page: mount-time history from\n52\t  `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1` (guarded behind `Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo)`\n53\t  so plain unit-test runs stay DB-free) + live `\"operational_logs\"` PubSub\n54\t  broadcasts of bare entry maps.\n55\t\n56\t  ## No sensitive data (spec §8 constraint 5)\n57\t\n58\t  The map, tooltips, aria-labels and the live announcer only ever carry\n59\t  route templates + counts. Log MESSAGE text appears only in the stream\n60\t  panel and the inspector's recent-events list — the same already-redacted\n61\t  `message` field the Live Stream page shows (LogStore's writer owns\n62\t  redaction) — never in tooltips/labels/announcements.\n63\t\n64\t  ## CSP (spec §8 constraint 2)\n65\t\n66\t  No inline JS anywhere. Client-only behavior (tooltip positioning, resizer\n67\t  drag/keyboard, Enter/Space activation of SVG nodes, stream autoscroll,\n68\t  war-room elapsed clock, status-transition pings) lives in the\n69\t  `TraceMapLeitstand` phx-hook in `server/priv/static/assets/app.js`;\n70\t  animations (error pulse, recording dot, pings) are pure CSS `@keyframes`\n71\t  in `server/assets/css/app.css`, disabled under `prefers-reduced-motion`.\n72\t  \"\"\"\n73\t  use Phoenix.LiveView, layout: {Nebu.Admin.Layouts, :live}\n74\t\n75\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapStatus\n76\t  alias Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.TraceMapTree\n77\t  alias Nebu.Admin.TraceMapGeometry\n78\t\n79\t  @topic \"operational_logs\"\n80\t  @base_path \"/admin/logs/trace-map\"\n81\t\n82\t  # UI level-pill vocabulary (spec §7) <-> LogStore level strings. The pill\n83\t  # labels are the spec's abbreviated forms; `Nebu.Web.LogStore` entries use\n84\t  # \"warning\"/\"error\" — mapped via ui_level/1 below.\n85\t  @ui_levels [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"]\n86\t\n87\t  @domain_labels %{\n88\t    \"/_matrix/client\" => \"Matrix Client\",\n89\t    \"/_matrix/media\" => \"Matrix Media\",\n90\t    \"/admin\" => \"Admin\",\n91\t    \"/health\" => \"Health\"\n92\t  }\n93\t\n94\t  # Bounded in-memory buffers (per-LiveView-process).\n95\t  @max_stream_lines 250\n96\t  @max_inspector_events 5\n97\t  @history_seed_cap 200\n98\t\n99\t  @tick_interval_ms 1_000\n100\t  @war_room_refresh_interval_ms 2_000\n101\t  @announce_debounce_ms 2_000\n102\t\n103\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n104\t  # Mount / handle_params\n105\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n106\t\n107\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n108\t  def mount(_params, _session, socket) do\n109\t    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(Nebu.PubSub, @topic)\n110\t\n111\t    socket =\n112\t      socket\n113\t      # admin-sidebar-nesting coordination (AC1 / UX gate advisory A2): the\n114\t      # Leitstand highlights the \"Trace Map\" CHILD of the nested Logs section.\n115\t      |> assign(:active_nav, \"logs-trace-map\")\n116\t      |> assign(:page_title, \"Trace Map\")\n117\t      |> assign(:endpoint_order, [])\n118\t      |> assign(:extra_domains, [])\n119\t      |> assign(:node_states, %{})\n120\t      |> assign(:node_events, %{})\n121\t      |> assign(:stream_entries, [])\n122\t      |> assign(:selected, [])\n123\t      |> assign(:active_levels, MapSet.new(@ui_levels))\n124\t      |> assign(:inspected, nil)\n125\t      |> assign(:war_room, initial_war_room_status())\n126\t      |> assign(:announcement, \"\")\n127\t      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, 0)\n128\t      |> assign(:now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))\n129\t\n130\t    socket =\n131\t      Enum.reduce(initial_entries(), socket, fn entry, acc ->\n132\t        ingest_entry(acc, entry, announce: false)\n133\t      end)\n134\t\n135\t    if connected?(socket) do\n136\t      schedule_tick()\n137\t      # `WarRoomManager.status/0` is a live DB read — only poll when\n138\t      # `Nebu.Repo` is running (mirrors `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive`'s guard).\n139\t      if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n140\t    end\n141\t\n142\t    {:ok, socket}\n143\t  end\n144\t\n145\t  # AC5: URL query params are the single source of truth for the endpoint\n146\t  # selection — seeded here on mount AND after every push_patch round trip.\n147\t  # A bookmarked URL may select endpoints/domains no traffic has been\n148\t  # observed for yet: those are added to the map (as satellite or\n149\t  # domain-only nodes) so the restored view renders its selection rings and\n150\t  # chips immediately (bookmarkable-URL contract, AC5/AC12 Scenario 10).\n151\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n152\t  def handle_params(params, _uri, socket) do\n153\t    selected = params |> Map.get(\"endpoint\") |> normalize_selected()\n154\t\n155\t    {endpoint_order, extra_domains} =\n156\t      Enum.reduce(selected, {socket.assigns.endpoint_order, socket.assigns.extra_domains}, fn key,\n157\t                                                                                              {order,\n158\t                                                                                               extras} ->\n159\t        cond do\n160\t          TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key ->\n161\t            # A key that IS its own domain (e.g. \"/admin\", \"/health\") is a\n162\t            # domain selection — render it as a domain node, never as a\n163\t            # synthetic satellite.\n164\t            if key in extras or Enum.any?(order, &(TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(&1) == key)) do\n165\t              {order, extras}\n166\t            else\n167\t              {order, extras ++ [key]}\n168\t            end\n169\t\n170\t          key in order ->\n171\t            {order, extras}\n172\t\n173\t          true ->\n174\t            {order ++ [key], extras}\n175\t        end\n176\t      end)\n177\t\n178\t    {:noreply,\n179\t     socket\n180\t     |> assign(:selected, selected)\n181\t     |> assign(:endpoint_order, endpoint_order)\n182\t     |> assign(:extra_domains, extra_domains)}\n183\t  end\n184\t\n185\t  defp normalize_selected(nil), do: []\n186\t  defp normalize_selected(value) when is_binary(value), do: [value]\n187\t\n188\t  defp normalize_selected(values) when is_list(values) do\n189\t    values |> Enum.filter(&is_binary/1) |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n190\t  end\n191\t\n192\t  defp normalize_selected(_other), do: []\n193\t\n194\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n195\t  # handle_event — node filter toggles (AC5) + level pills (AC9)\n196\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n197\t\n198\t  # An ordinary server round trip (unlike the Live Stream page's\n199\t  # client-side-instant bubbles — no zero-lag constraint applies here, per\n200\t  # the ATDD contract): toggle the key in the selection, then push_patch so\n201\t  # handle_params/3 re-seeds @selected from the URL. The clicked node also\n202\t  # becomes the inspected node (AC8; mirrors the dummy's `activate()`).\n203\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n204\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", %{\"key\" => key}, socket) when is_binary(key) do\n205\t    selected = socket.assigns.selected\n206\t\n207\t    new_selected =\n208\t      if key in selected, do: List.delete(selected, key), else: selected ++ [key]\n209\t\n210\t    {:noreply,\n211\t     socket\n212\t     |> assign(:inspected, key)\n213\t     |> push_patch(to: patch_path(new_selected))}\n214\t  end\n215\t\n216\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_node\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n217\t\n218\t  # Clicking nebu (or the stream header's \"Clear all\") clears every filter.\n219\t  def handle_event(\"clear_filters\", _params, socket) do\n220\t    {:noreply, push_patch(socket, to: patch_path([]))}\n221\t  end\n222\t\n223\t  # AC9's leitstand shortcut: all active + click one -> ONLY that one;\n224\t  # the sole active one clicked again -> all active; otherwise plain toggle.\n225\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", %{\"level\" => level}, socket) when level in @ui_levels do\n226\t    active = socket.assigns.active_levels\n227\t    all = MapSet.new(@ui_levels)\n228\t\n229\t    new_active =\n230\t      cond do\n231\t        MapSet.equal?(active, all) -> MapSet.new([level])\n232\t        MapSet.equal?(active, MapSet.new([level])) -> all\n233\t        MapSet.member?(active, level) -> MapSet.delete(active, level)\n234\t        true -> MapSet.put(active, level)\n235\t      end\n236\t\n237\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :active_levels, new_active)}\n238\t  end\n239\t\n240\t  def handle_event(\"toggle_level\", _params, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n241\t\n242\t  # AC5 URL format (story amendment): bracket-array `endpoint[]=...` params,\n243\t  # percent-encoded (`endpoint%5B%5D=...`) — `Plug.Conn.Query.decode/1`\n244\t  # natively decodes this into `%{\"endpoint\" => [...]}` with no data loss.\n245\t  defp patch_path([]), do: @base_path\n246\t\n247\t  defp patch_path(selected) do\n248\t    query = Enum.map_join(selected, \"&\", fn key -> \"endpoint%5B%5D=\" <> URI.encode_www_form(key) end)\n249\t    @base_path <> \"?\" <> query\n250\t  end\n251\t\n252\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n253\t  # handle_info — live PubSub ingest (AC3) + tick (auto-heal) + war-room poll\n254\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n255\t\n256\t  # Advancing `@now_ms` every second is what makes the AC3 window actually\n257\t  # SLIDE for the reader: statuses/counts are pure functions of\n258\t  # `(state, now_ms)` (see TraceMapStatus), so a node with no new traffic\n259\t  # auto-heals to :ok the moment its last warning/error falls out of the\n260\t  # window — no acknowledge, no cleanup pass, just a fresher `now`.\n261\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n262\t  def handle_info(:trace_map_tick, socket) do\n263\t    schedule_tick()\n264\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :now_ms, System.system_time(:millisecond))}\n265\t  end\n266\t\n267\t  # AC6: the banner reacts to sessions starting/stopping while the page is\n268\t  # open. Always rescheduled (unlike the Live Stream page's\n269\t  # only-while-active refresh) because the banner must also APPEAR when a\n270\t  # session starts later.\n271\t  #\n272\t  # UX gate V1: the banner itself is not a live region (it contains a\n273\t  # per-second clock) — a session START/STOP transition is announced as a\n274\t  # one-shot state-change sentence through the sr-only announcer instead.\n275\t  def handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status, socket) do\n276\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo), do: schedule_war_room_refresh()\n277\t\n278\t    previous = socket.assigns.war_room\n279\t    current = initial_war_room_status()\n280\t\n281\t    socket =\n282\t      case {previous, current} do\n283\t        {:none, {:ok, session}} ->\n284\t          assign(socket, :announcement, \"War Room session started — capturing \" <> session.started_by)\n285\t\n286\t        {{:ok, _session}, :none} ->\n287\t          assign(socket, :announcement, \"War Room session ended\")\n288\t\n289\t        _unchanged ->\n290\t          socket\n291\t      end\n292\t\n293\t    {:noreply, assign(socket, :war_room, current)}\n294\t  end\n295\t\n296\t  # `Nebu.Web.LogStore.Handler.safe_broadcast/1` broadcasts a BARE MAP (not a\n297\t  # tagged tuple) — same match as `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive.handle_info/2`.\n298\t  def handle_info(%{level: _, source: _, request_id: _} = entry, socket) do\n299\t    {:noreply, ingest_entry(socket, entry, announce: true)}\n300\t  end\n301\t\n302\t  def handle_info(_other, socket), do: {:noreply, socket}\n303\t\n304\t  defp schedule_tick, do: Process.send_after(self(), :trace_map_tick, @tick_interval_ms)\n305\t\n306\t  defp schedule_war_room_refresh do\n307\t    Process.send_after(self(), :refresh_war_room_status, @war_room_refresh_interval_ms)\n308\t  end\n309\t\n310\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n311\t  # Ingest\n312\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n313\t\n314\t  defp ingest_entry(socket, entry, opts) do\n315\t    level = normalize_level(entry[:level])\n316\t    route = normalize_route(entry[:route_template])\n317\t    {ts_ms, ts_text} = entry_timestamp(entry)\n318\t\n319\t    node_keys =\n320\t      case route do\n321\t        nil -> [\"nebu\"]\n322\t        route -> [route, TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route), \"nebu\"]\n323\t      end\n324\t\n325\t    socket\n326\t    |> track_endpoint(route)\n327\t    |> record_event(node_keys, level, ts_ms)\n328\t    |> push_node_event(node_keys, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n329\t    |> push_stream_line(route, level, ts_text, entry[:message])\n330\t    |> maybe_announce(route, level, ts_ms, ts_text, opts)\n331\t    |> assign(:now_ms, max(ts_ms, socket.assigns.now_ms))\n332\t  end\n333\t\n334\t  defp track_endpoint(socket, nil), do: socket\n335\t\n336\t  defp track_endpoint(socket, route) do\n337\t    update(socket, :endpoint_order, fn order ->\n338\t      if route in order, do: order, else: order ++ [route]\n339\t    end)\n340\t  end\n341\t\n342\t  # Every entry counts as a :request in its node's window; warnings/errors\n343\t  # are ADDITIONALLY recorded so the badge/tooltip counts match the dummy's\n344\t  # semantics (total = all events, warn/err = level subsets).\n345\t  defp record_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_ms) do\n346\t    kinds =\n347\t      case level do\n348\t        \"error\" -> [:request, :error]\n349\t        \"warning\" -> [:request, :warning]\n350\t        _ -> [:request]\n351\t      end\n352\t\n353\t    update(socket, :node_states, fn states ->\n354\t      Enum.reduce(node_keys, states, fn key, acc ->\n355\t        state = Map.get(acc, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n356\t        state = Enum.reduce(kinds, state, &TraceMapStatus.record(&2, &1, ts_ms))\n357\t        Map.put(acc, key, state)\n358\t      end)\n359\t    end)\n360\t  end\n361\t\n362\t  defp push_node_event(socket, node_keys, level, ts_text, message) do\n363\t    event = %{ts: ts_text, level: ui_level(level), message: truncate(message)}\n364\t\n365\t    update(socket, :node_events, fn events ->\n366\t      Enum.reduce(node_keys, events, fn key, acc ->\n367\t        Map.update(acc, key, [event], &Enum.take([event | &1], @max_inspector_events))\n368\t      end)\n369\t    end)\n370\t  end\n371\t\n372\t  defp push_stream_line(socket, route, level, ts_text, message) do\n373\t    line = %{\n374\t      ts: ts_text,\n375\t      ui_level: ui_level(level),\n376\t      route: route,\n377\t      domain: route && TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route),\n378\t      message: truncate(message)\n379\t    }\n380\t\n381\t    update(socket, :stream_entries, fn lines ->\n382\t      (lines ++ [line]) |> Enum.take(-@max_stream_lines)\n383\t    end)\n384\t  end\n385\t\n386\t  # AC10: aria-live announcements for new errors — throttled (2s debounce),\n387\t  # endpoint name + windowed count, no message content (spec §8's\n388\t  # no-sensitive-data rule). UX gate A2: the count and the timestamp are part\n389\t  # of the sentence so (a) the announcement matches AC10's own example\n390\t  # (\"3 new errors detected in sync endpoint\") and (b) a REPEATED error burst\n391\t  # on the same endpoint after the debounce still produces a DOM change —\n392\t  # identical text would never re-fire a polite live region.\n393\t  defp maybe_announce(socket, route, \"error\", ts_ms, ts_text, opts) do\n394\t    if Keyword.get(opts, :announce, true) and is_binary(route) and\n395\t         ts_ms - socket.assigns.last_announce_ms >= @announce_debounce_ms do\n396\t      errors = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(socket.assigns.node_states, route), ts_ms).errors\n397\t\n398\t      socket\n399\t      |> assign(\n400\t        :announcement,\n401\t        \"#{pluralize(errors, \"new error\")} detected in #{route} at #{ts_text}\"\n402\t      )\n403\t      |> assign(:last_announce_ms, ts_ms)\n404\t    else\n405\t      socket\n406\t    end\n407\t  end\n408\t\n409\t  defp maybe_announce(socket, _route, _level, _ts_ms, _ts_text, _opts), do: socket\n410\t\n411\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n412\t  # Data-source guards (DB-free unit-test contract, see moduledoc)\n413\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n414\t\n415\t  defp initial_war_room_status do\n416\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n417\t      Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.status()\n418\t    else\n419\t      :none\n420\t    end\n421\t  end\n422\t\n423\t  defp initial_entries do\n424\t    if Process.whereis(Nebu.Repo) do\n425\t      {:ok, rows} = Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs(%{})\n426\t\n427\t      # most-recent-first from get_logs/1; reverse to oldest-first so the\n428\t      # stream panel reads chronologically and inspector events stack\n429\t      # newest-first correctly.\n430\t      rows |> Enum.take(@history_seed_cap) |> Enum.reverse()\n431\t    else\n432\t      []\n433\t    end\n434\t  end\n435\t\n436\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n437\t  # Normalization helpers\n438\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n439\t\n440\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_binary(level), do: level\n441\t  defp normalize_level(level) when is_atom(level) and not is_nil(level), do: Atom.to_string(level)\n442\t  defp normalize_level(_), do: \"info\"\n443\t\n444\t  defp normalize_route(nil), do: nil\n445\t  defp normalize_route(\"\"), do: nil\n446\t  defp normalize_route(route) when is_binary(route), do: route\n447\t  defp normalize_route(route), do: to_string(route)\n448\t\n449\t  defp ui_level(\"debug\"), do: \"DEBUG\"\n450\t  defp ui_level(\"warning\"), do: \"WARN\"\n451\t  defp ui_level(\"error\"), do: \"ERROR\"\n452\t  defp ui_level(_), do: \"INFO\"\n453\t\n454\t  defp truncate(nil), do: \"\"\n455\t\n456\t  defp truncate(message) when is_binary(message) do\n457\t    if String.length(message) > 120, do: String.slice(message, 0, 120) <> \"…\", else: message\n458\t  end\n459\t\n460\t  defp truncate(message), do: truncate(to_string(message))\n461\t\n462\t  # Live broadcasts carry no timestamp -> \"now\"; mount-time LogStore history\n463\t  # rows carry `inserted_at` (DateTime/NaiveDateTime) -> converted so the\n464\t  # 60-second window judges seeded rows by their REAL age, not by mount time.\n465\t  defp entry_timestamp(entry) do\n466\t    case entry[:inserted_at] do\n467\t      %DateTime{} = dt ->\n468\t        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n469\t\n470\t      %NaiveDateTime{} = naive ->\n471\t        dt = DateTime.from_naive!(naive, \"Etc/UTC\")\n472\t        {DateTime.to_unix(dt, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(dt, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n473\t\n474\t      _ ->\n475\t        now = DateTime.utc_now()\n476\t        {DateTime.to_unix(now, :millisecond), Calendar.strftime(now, \"%H:%M:%S\")}\n477\t    end\n478\t  end\n479\t\n480\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n481\t  # Render\n482\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n483\t\n484\t  @impl Phoenix.LiveView\n485\t  def render(assigns) do\n486\t    groups = domain_groups(assigns.endpoint_order, assigns.extra_domains)\n487\t\n488\t    layout =\n489\t      TraceMapGeometry.layout(\n490\t        Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} -> %{key: domain, satellites: leaves} end)\n491\t      )\n492\t\n493\t    assigns =\n494\t      assigns\n495\t      |> assign(:nodes, build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns))\n496\t      |> assign(:edges, build_edges(groups, layout))\n497\t      |> assign(:visible_lines, visible_lines(assigns))\n498\t      |> assign(:inspected_node, inspected_node(assigns))\n499\t      |> assign(:ui_levels, @ui_levels)\n500\t\n501\t    ~H\"\"\"\n502\t    <div\n503\t      id=\"trace-map-leitstand\"\n504\t      phx-hook=\"TraceMapLeitstand\"\n505\t      class=\"flex flex-col gap-3\"\n506\t      style=\"height: calc(100vh - 6.5rem);\"\n507\t    >\n508\t      <div class=\"flex items-center justify-between shrink-0\">\n509\t        <h1 class=\"text-heading font-semibold\">Trace Map</h1>\n510\t      </div>\n511\t\n512\t      <%!-- AC6: war-room banner — always present in markup, toggled via the\n513\t            boolean `hidden` attribute. Deliberately NOT a live region (UX gate\n514\t            V1): it contains the per-second-ticking elapsed clock, and\n515\t            role=\"status\" (implicitly aria-atomic) would make screen readers\n516\t            re-announce the whole banner every second. Session start/stop is\n517\t            announced as a one-shot sentence through the sr-only announcer at\n518\t            the bottom of the page instead (see handle_info\n519\t            :refresh_war_room_status). --%>\n520\t      <%!-- No `flex` utility here, deliberately: an author-stylesheet\n521\t            `.flex { display: flex }` beats the UA's `[hidden] { display: none }`\n522\t            in the cascade, so the \"hidden\" banner would still render (the exact\n523\t            trace-map-hierarchy/admin-sidebar-nesting flex-beats-hidden bug —\n524\t            reconfirmed live in dev cycle 1). `.tm-wr-banner` owns display:flex\n525\t            AND a `[hidden]` override in app.css. --%>\n526\t      <div\n527\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"\n528\t        hidden={not war_room_active?(@war_room)}\n529\t        class=\"tm-wr-banner items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border border-error bg-status-error-bg px-4 py-2 text-sm shrink-0\"\n530\t      >\n531\t        <span data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\" class=\"tm-recdot\" aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n532\t        <span :if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}>\n533\t          War Room session active — capturing\n534\t          <b class=\"font-mono font-medium\">{war_room_session(@war_room).started_by}</b>\n535\t          · <span\n536\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n537\t            id=\"trace-map-war-room-elapsed\"\n538\t            data-started-at={war_room_started_at_unix(@war_room)}\n539\t          >{war_room_elapsed(@war_room)}</span>\n540\t          <span :if={war_room_session(@war_room).reason} class=\"text-text-secondary\">\n541\t            · {war_room_session(@war_room).reason}\n542\t          </span>\n543\t        </span>\n544\t        <.link\n545\t          navigate=\"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n546\t          data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"\n547\t          class=\"btn btn-outline btn-xs ml-auto border-error text-status-error-text hover:bg-error hover:text-error-content hover:border-error\"\n548\t        >\n549\t          Open War Room\n550\t        </.link>\n551\t      </div>\n552\t\n553\t      <div class=\"flex gap-4 flex-1 min-h-0\">\n554\t        <div class=\"flex-1 flex flex-col min-w-0\" id=\"trace-map-left\">\n555\t          <div class=\"relative flex-1 min-h-0 rounded-lg bg-base-400 border border-base-300\" id=\"trace-map-map-wrap\">\n556\t            <svg\n557\t              data-testid=\"trace-map-svg\"\n558\t              viewBox=\"-560 -400 1120 800\"\n559\t              preserveAspectRatio=\"xMidYMid meet\"\n560\t              role=\"group\"\n561\t              aria-label=\"Service health map\"\n562\t              class=\"w-full h-full block\"\n563\t            >\n564\t              <g>\n565\t                <line\n566\t                  :for={edge <- @edges}\n567\t                  class=\"tm-edge\"\n568\t                  x1={edge.x1}\n569\t                  y1={edge.y1}\n570\t                  x2={edge.x2}\n571\t                  y2={edge.y2}\n572\t                />\n573\t              </g>\n574\t              <g id=\"trace-map-pings\"></g>\n575\t              <g>\n576\t                <.map_node :for={node <- @nodes} node={node} />\n577\t              </g>\n578\t            </svg>\n579\t            <%!-- UX gate A5: aria-hidden — the tooltip only duplicates the\n580\t                  hovered/focused node's own aria-label content for sighted\n581\t                  users; virtual-cursor users would otherwise meet it twice. --%>\n582\t            <div\n583\t              data-testid=\"trace-map-tooltip\"\n584\t              id=\"trace-map-tooltip\"\n585\t              class=\"tm-tooltip\"\n586\t              aria-hidden=\"true\"\n587\t              hidden\n588\t            >\n589\t            </div>\n590\t          </div>\n591\t\n592\t          <%!-- AC9: resizable drag handle — pointer drag + ArrowUp/Down ±32px,\n593\t                Home/End max/min, dblclick resets to 218px (TraceMapLeitstand hook). --%>\n594\t          <%!-- UX gate V4: a focusable role=\"separator\" requires\n595\t                aria-valuenow (ARIA window-splitter pattern). The server\n596\t                renders an approximate initial value (218px default stream on\n597\t                a typical column); the TraceMapLeitstand hook recomputes the\n598\t                exact percentage on mount and on every resize. --%>\n599\t          <div\n600\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n601\t            id=\"trace-map-resizer\"\n602\t            role=\"separator\"\n603\t            aria-orientation=\"horizontal\"\n604\t            tabindex=\"0\"\n605\t            aria-valuemin=\"0\"\n606\t            aria-valuemax=\"100\"\n607\t            aria-valuenow=\"30\"\n608\t            aria-label=\"Resize log stream (drag, or arrow up/down; double-click resets)\"\n609\t            class=\"tm-resizer shrink-0\"\n610\t          >\n611\t          </div>\n612\t\n613\t          <section\n614\t            data-testid=\"trace-map-stream\"\n615\t            id=\"trace-map-stream\"\n616\t            aria-label=\"Live log stream\"\n617\t            class=\"tm-stream flex flex-col shrink-0 border border-base-300 rounded-b-lg bg-base-100\"\n618\t          >\n619\t            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap px-3 py-2 border-b border-base-300 shrink-0\">\n620\t              <span class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary\">Live stream</span>\n621\t              <span role=\"group\" aria-label=\"Level filter\" class=\"inline-flex gap-1\">\n622\t                <button\n623\t                  :for={level <- @ui_levels}\n624\t                  type=\"button\"\n625\t                  data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"\n626\t                  data-level={level}\n627\t                  aria-pressed={to_string(MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))}\n628\t                  phx-click=\"toggle_level\"\n629\t                  phx-value-level={level}\n630\t                  class={[\n631\t                    \"font-mono text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border\",\n632\t                    \"focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary focus-visible:outline-offset-2\",\n633\t                    level_pill_class(level, MapSet.member?(@active_levels, level))\n634\t                  ]}\n635\t                >\n636\t                  {level}\n637\t                </button>\n638\t              </span>\n639\t              <span\n640\t                :for={key <- @selected}\n641\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip\"\n642\t                data-chip-value={key}\n643\t                class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-xs border border-primary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 bg-base-200\"\n644\t              >\n645\t                {key}\n646\t                <button\n647\t                  type=\"button\"\n648\t                  data-testid=\"trace-map-filter-chip-remove\"\n649\t                  phx-click=\"toggle_node\"\n650\t                  phx-value-key={key}\n651\t                  aria-label={\"Remove filter \" <> key}\n652\t                  class=\"text-primary font-bold px-0.5 rounded focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary focus-visible:outline-offset-2\"\n653\t                >\n654\t                  ×\n655\t                </button>\n656\t              </span>\n657\t              <button\n658\t                :if={@selected != []}\n659\t                type=\"button\"\n660\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-clear-all\"\n661\t                phx-click=\"clear_filters\"\n662\t                class=\"inline-flex items-center font-mono text-xs border border-base-300 text-text-secondary rounded-full px-2 py-0.5 hover:bg-base-300 focus-visible:outline focus-visible:outline-2 focus-visible:outline-primary focus-visible:outline-offset-2\"\n663\t              >\n664\t                Clear all\n665\t              </button>\n666\t            </div>\n667\t            <%!-- UX gate A5: the tabbable scroll container gets an accessible\n668\t                  name. Deliberately NOT role=\"log\" — that role carries an\n669\t                  implicit polite live region, which would announce every\n670\t                  incoming line on a high-volume stream. --%>\n671\t            <div\n672\t              id=\"trace-map-stream-body\"\n673\t              aria-label=\"Log stream lines\"\n674\t              class=\"flex-1 overflow-y-auto px-3 py-1 font-mono text-xs\"\n675\t              tabindex=\"0\"\n676\t            >\n677\t              <div\n678\t                :for={line <- @visible_lines}\n679\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"\n680\t                data-level={line.ui_level}\n681\t                data-route={line.route || \"\"}\n682\t                class=\"flex gap-2 whitespace-nowrap py-px\"\n683\t              >\n684\t                <span class=\"text-text-secondary\">{line.ts}</span>\n685\t                <span class={[\"w-12 shrink-0 font-bold\", level_text_class(line.ui_level)]}>{line.ui_level}</span>\n686\t                <span class=\"text-base-content\">{line.route}</span>\n687\t                <span class=\"text-text-secondary\">{line.message}</span>\n688\t              </div>\n689\t            </div>\n690\t          </section>\n691\t        </div>\n692\t\n693\t        <%!-- AC8: sticky right column — inspector card + always-visible legend. --%>\n694\t        <aside class=\"w-80 shrink-0 flex flex-col gap-3 overflow-y-auto\">\n695\t          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n696\t            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Node inspector</h2>\n697\t            <p :if={is_nil(@inspected_node)} data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-empty\" class=\"text-sm text-text-secondary\">\n698\t              Hover or select a node to see its live health details.\n699\t            </p>\n700\t            <div :if={@inspected_node}>\n701\t              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-name\" class=\"font-semibold text-base\">\n702\t                {@inspected_node.label}\n703\t              </div>\n704\t              <div data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-route\" class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary break-all mb-2\">\n705\t                {@inspected_node.route}\n706\t              </div>\n707\t              <span\n708\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-status-pill\"\n709\t                data-status={@inspected_node.status}\n710\t                class={[\n711\t                  \"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 mb-3\",\n712\t                  status_pill_class(@inspected_node.status)\n713\t                ]}\n714\t              >\n715\t                {status_pill_text(@inspected_node.status)}\n716\t              </span>\n717\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n718\t                <span>Requests / 60 s</span>\n719\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-requests\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n720\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.requests}\n721\t                </b>\n722\t              </div>\n723\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n724\t                <span>Warnings / 60 s</span>\n725\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-warnings\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n726\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.warnings}\n727\t                </b>\n728\t              </div>\n729\t              <div class=\"flex justify-between text-sm text-text-secondary py-0.5\">\n730\t                <span>Errors / 60 s</span>\n731\t                <b data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-stat-errors\" class=\"font-mono font-medium text-base-content\">\n732\t                  {@inspected_node.counts.errors}\n733\t                </b>\n734\t              </div>\n735\t              <h3 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mt-3 mb-1\">Recent events</h3>\n736\t              <p :if={@inspected_node.events == []} class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary\">—</p>\n737\t              <div\n738\t                :for={event <- @inspected_node.events}\n739\t                data-testid=\"trace-map-inspector-event\"\n740\t                class=\"font-mono text-xs text-text-secondary py-px\"\n741\t              >\n742\t                {event.ts} · {event.level} · {event.message}\n743\t              </div>\n744\t            </div>\n745\t          </div>\n746\t\n747\t          <div data-testid=\"trace-map-legend\" class=\"rounded-lg border border-base-300 bg-base-200 p-4\">\n748\t            <h2 class=\"text-caption uppercase text-text-secondary font-semibold mb-2\">Legend</h2>\n749\t            <div\n750\t              :for={{state, dot_class, text} <- legend_rows()}\n751\t              data-testid=\"trace-map-legend-row\"\n752\t              data-legend-state={state}\n753\t              class=\"flex items-center gap-2.5 py-1 text-sm text-text-secondary\"\n754\t            >\n755\t              <span class={[\"tm-legend-dot\", dot_class]} aria-hidden=\"true\"></span>\n756\t              <span>{text}</span>\n757\t            </div>\n758\t            <p class=\"text-xs text-text-secondary mt-2\">\n759\t              Domains inherit the worst status of their satellites; nebu inherits the worst domain.\n760\t              Nodes heal automatically once the 60 s window passes.\n761\t            </p>\n762\t          </div>\n763\t        </aside>\n764\t      </div>\n765\t\n766\t      <%!-- AC10: throttled aria-live announcer for new errors (endpoint name\n767\t            + no message content — spec §8). --%>\n768\t      <div data-testid=\"trace-map-announcer\" role=\"status\" aria-live=\"polite\" class=\"sr-only\">\n769\t        {@announcement}\n770\t      </div>\n771\t    </div>\n772\t    \"\"\"\n773\t  end\n774\t\n775\t  # ── One SVG map node (nebu / domain / satellite) ──────────────────────────\n776\t  #\n777\t  # Focusable `role=\"button\"` per AC10; Enter/Space activation is wired by\n778\t  # the TraceMapLeitstand hook (SVG <g> has no native keyboard activation).\n779\t  # `aria-pressed` reflects selection; nebu is never a toggle target (always\n780\t  # \"false\" — clicking it CLEARS filters instead).\n781\t  attr(:node, :map, required: true)\n782\t\n783\t  defp map_node(assigns) do\n784\t    ~H\"\"\"\n785\t    <g\n786\t      id={@node.dom_id}\n787\t      data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"\n788\t      data-node-key={@node.key}\n789\t      data-node-kind={@node.kind}\n790\t      data-node-status={@node.status}\n791\t      data-node-selected={to_string(@node.selected)}\n792\t      role=\"button\"\n793\t      tabindex=\"0\"\n794\t      aria-pressed={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && to_string(@node.pressed)}\n795\t      aria-label={@node.aria_label}\n796\t      data-tooltip-name={@node.label}\n797\t      data-tooltip-route={@node.route}\n798\t      data-tooltip-status={@node.tooltip_status}\n799\t      data-tooltip-counts={@node.tooltip_counts}\n800\t      data-tooltip-cta={@node.tooltip_cta}\n801\t      transform={\"translate(#{@node.x} #{@node.y})\"}\n802\t      phx-click={@node.click_event}\n803\t      phx-value-key={@node.kind != \"nebu\" && @node.key}\n804\t      class={\"tm-node tm-node--\" <> @node.status}\n805\t    >\n806\t      <circle class=\"tm-selring\" r={@node.r + 7} />\n807\t      <circle data-testid=\"trace-map-node-core\" class=\"tm-node-core\" r={@node.r} />\n808\t      <%= case @node.kind do %>\n809\t        <% \"nebu\" -> %>\n810\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-2\">nebu</text>\n811\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"16\">{@node.counts.requests}/min</text>\n812\t        <% \"domain\" -> %>\n813\t          <text class=\"tm-nname\" y=\"-1\">{@node.label}</text>\n814\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"14\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n815\t        <% _satellite -> %>\n816\t          <text class=\"tm-ncount\" y=\"4\">{@node.counts.requests}</text>\n817\t          <text class=\"tm-nlabel\" y={@node.r + 15}>{@node.label}</text>\n818\t      <% end %>\n819\t      <g\n820\t        :if={@node.status == \"warn\"}\n821\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-warn\"\n822\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--warn\"\n823\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n824\t      >\n825\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n826\t        <text y=\"3.5\">▲{badge_count(@node.counts.warnings)}</text>\n827\t      </g>\n828\t      <g\n829\t        :if={@node.status == \"error\"}\n830\t        data-testid=\"trace-map-badge-error\"\n831\t        class=\"tm-badge tm-badge--error\"\n832\t        transform={badge_transform(@node.r)}\n833\t      >\n834\t        <circle r=\"9\" />\n835\t        <text y=\"3.5\">✕{badge_count(@node.counts.errors)}</text>\n836\t      </g>\n837\t    </g>\n838\t    \"\"\"\n839\t  end\n840\t\n841\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n842\t  # View-model builders\n843\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n844\t\n845\t  # Groups observed endpoints by domain (REUSING TraceMapTree's canonical\n846\t  # heuristic), then appends URL-selected domains no traffic has produced a\n847\t  # satellite for yet.\n848\t  defp domain_groups(endpoint_order, extra_domains) do\n849\t    groups = TraceMapTree.group_endpoints_by_domain(endpoint_order)\n850\t    known = Enum.map(groups, fn {domain, _leaves} -> domain end)\n851\t\n852\t    groups ++ (extra_domains |> Enum.reject(&(&1 in known)) |> Enum.map(&{&1, []}))\n853\t  end\n854\t\n855\t  # DOM order (AC10 natural Tab order): nebu first, then each domain followed\n856\t  # by its own satellites.\n857\t  defp build_nodes(groups, layout, assigns) do\n858\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n859\t    states = assigns.node_states\n860\t    selected = assigns.selected\n861\t\n862\t    satellite_statuses =\n863\t      Map.new(layout.satellites, fn {key, _pos} ->\n864\t        {key, TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, key), now)}\n865\t      end)\n866\t\n867\t    domain_statuses =\n868\t      Map.new(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n869\t        child_statuses = Enum.map(leaves, &Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, &1))\n870\t        own = TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, domain), now)\n871\t        {domain, TraceMapStatus.worst([own | child_statuses])}\n872\t      end)\n873\t\n874\t    nebu_status =\n875\t      TraceMapStatus.worst([\n876\t        TraceMapStatus.status(node_state(states, \"nebu\"), now)\n877\t        | Map.values(domain_statuses)\n878\t      ])\n879\t\n880\t    nebu_node =\n881\t      build_node(\"nebu\", \"nebu\", layout.nebu, nebu_status, \"nebu\", \"all traffic\", selected, states, now)\n882\t\n883\t    domain_and_satellite_nodes =\n884\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n885\t        domain_node =\n886\t          build_node(\n887\t            domain,\n888\t            \"domain\",\n889\t            Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain),\n890\t            Map.fetch!(domain_statuses, domain),\n891\t            domain_label(domain),\n892\t            domain,\n893\t            selected,\n894\t            states,\n895\t            now\n896\t          )\n897\t\n898\t        satellite_nodes =\n899\t          Enum.map(leaves, fn route ->\n900\t            build_node(\n901\t              route,\n902\t              \"satellite\",\n903\t              Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route),\n904\t              Map.fetch!(satellite_statuses, route),\n905\t              satellite_label(route),\n906\t              route,\n907\t              selected,\n908\t              states,\n909\t              now\n910\t            )\n911\t          end)\n912\t\n913\t        [domain_node | satellite_nodes]\n914\t      end)\n915\t\n916\t    [nebu_node | domain_and_satellite_nodes]\n917\t  end\n918\t\n919\t  defp build_node(key, kind, position, status, label, route, selected, states, now) do\n920\t    counts = TraceMapStatus.counts(node_state(states, key), now)\n921\t    status_string = Atom.to_string(status)\n922\t    is_selected = kind != \"nebu\" and key in selected\n923\t\n924\t    %{\n925\t      key: key,\n926\t      kind: kind,\n927\t      # A stable DOM id per node: LiveView's DOM patching matches keyed\n928\t      # elements in place, so a selection/status patch can never replace the\n929\t      # <g> element itself — keyboard focus survives Enter/Space toggles\n930\t      # (AC10; verified against the live stack in dev cycle 1).\n931\t      #\n932\t      # KIND-prefixed (dev cycle 2): \"/health\" is simultaneously a domain key\n933\t      # AND a satellite key (a single-segment route is its own domain per\n934\t      # TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint/1) — a key-only id produced two\n935\t      # elements with the SAME id, which corrupts LiveView's id-keyed DOM\n936\t      # patching for exactly those nodes (observed live: the toggle patch for\n937\t      # the /health domain never applied).\n938\t      dom_id: \"tm-node-\" <> kind <> \"-\" <> Base.url_encode64(key, padding: false),\n939\t      x: coord(position.x),\n940\t      y: coord(position.y),\n941\t      r: position.r,\n942\t      status: status_string,\n943\t      selected: is_selected,\n944\t      pressed: is_selected,\n945\t      label: label,\n946\t      route: route,\n947\t      counts: counts,\n948\t      aria_label: node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts),\n949\t      tooltip_status: status_word(status),\n950\t      tooltip_counts: counts_text(counts),\n951\t      tooltip_cta: tooltip_cta(kind),\n952\t      click_event: if(kind == \"nebu\", do: \"clear_filters\", else: \"toggle_node\")\n953\t    }\n954\t  end\n955\t\n956\t  defp node_state(states, key), do: Map.get(states, key, TraceMapStatus.new())\n957\t\n958\t  defp build_edges(groups, layout) do\n959\t    domain_edges =\n960\t      Enum.map(layout.domains, fn {_key, domain} ->\n961\t        edge(layout.nebu, domain)\n962\t      end)\n963\t\n964\t    satellite_edges =\n965\t      Enum.flat_map(groups, fn {domain, leaves} ->\n966\t        domain_pos = Map.fetch!(layout.domains, domain)\n967\t        Enum.map(leaves, fn route -> edge(domain_pos, Map.fetch!(layout.satellites, route)) end)\n968\t      end)\n969\t\n970\t    domain_edges ++ satellite_edges\n971\t  end\n972\t\n973\t  defp edge(a, b) do\n974\t    {x1, y1, x2, y2} = TraceMapGeometry.trim_edge(a, b)\n975\t    %{x1: coord(x1), y1: coord(y1), x2: coord(x2), y2: coord(y2)}\n976\t  end\n977\t\n978\t  defp coord(value), do: :erlang.float_to_binary(value / 1, decimals: 2)\n979\t\n980\t  defp visible_lines(assigns) do\n981\t    Enum.filter(assigns.stream_entries, fn line ->\n982\t      MapSet.member?(assigns.active_levels, line.ui_level) and\n983\t        endpoint_match?(line, assigns.selected)\n984\t    end)\n985\t  end\n986\t\n987\t  # AC5: OR within the endpoint dimension; a selected DOMAIN matches all its\n988\t  # satellites' lines.\n989\t  defp endpoint_match?(_line, []), do: true\n990\t\n991\t  defp endpoint_match?(line, selected) do\n992\t    line.route in selected or (line.domain != nil and line.domain in selected)\n993\t  end\n994\t\n995\t  defp inspected_node(%{inspected: nil}), do: nil\n996\t\n997\t  defp inspected_node(assigns) do\n998\t    key = assigns.inspected\n999\t    now = assigns.now_ms\n1000\t    state = node_state(assigns.node_states, key)\n1001\t\n1002\t    {label, route} =\n1003\t      cond do\n1004\t        key == \"nebu\" -> {\"nebu\", \"all traffic\"}\n1005\t        TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(key) == key -> {domain_label(key), key}\n1006\t        true -> {satellite_label(key), key}\n1007\t      end\n1008\t\n1009\t    %{\n1010\t      label: label,\n1011\t      route: route,\n1012\t      status: state |> TraceMapStatus.status(now) |> Atom.to_string(),\n1013\t      counts: TraceMapStatus.counts(state, now),\n1014\t      events: Map.get(assigns.node_events, key, [])\n1015\t    }\n1016\t  end\n1017\t\n1018\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1019\t  # Labels / text helpers\n1020\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1021\t\n1022\t  defp domain_label(domain), do: Map.get(@domain_labels, domain, domain)\n1023\t\n1024\t  defp satellite_label(route) do\n1025\t    route\n1026\t    |> String.split(\"/\")\n1027\t    |> Enum.reject(&(&1 == \"\"))\n1028\t    |> List.last()\n1029\t    |> case do\n1030\t      nil -> route\n1031\t      segment -> segment\n1032\t    end\n1033\t  end\n1034\t\n1035\t  # AC10: name + route + status + counts, e.g.\n1036\t  # \"users endpoint · /admin/users · errors · 1 error · 0 warnings · 1 request in the last 60 seconds\"\n1037\t  defp node_aria_label(kind, label, route, status, counts) do\n1038\t    name =\n1039\t      case kind do\n1040\t        \"nebu\" -> \"nebu\"\n1041\t        \"domain\" -> label <> \" domain\"\n1042\t        _ -> label <> \" endpoint\"\n1043\t      end\n1044\t\n1045\t    Enum.join(\n1046\t      [\n1047\t        name,\n1048\t        route,\n1049\t        status_word(status),\n1050\t        pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\"),\n1051\t        pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\"),\n1052\t        pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\") <> \" in the last 60 seconds\"\n1053\t      ],\n1054\t      \" · \"\n1055\t    )\n1056\t  end\n1057\t\n1058\t  defp status_word(:ok), do: \"healthy\"\n1059\t  defp status_word(:warn), do: \"warnings\"\n1060\t  defp status_word(:error), do: \"errors\"\n1061\t\n1062\t  defp counts_text(counts) do\n1063\t    \"#{pluralize(counts.requests, \"request\")} · #{pluralize(counts.warnings, \"warning\")} · \" <>\n1064\t      \"#{pluralize(counts.errors, \"error\")} in the last 60s\"\n1065\t  end\n1066\t\n1067\t  defp pluralize(1, word), do: \"1 \" <> word\n1068\t  defp pluralize(n, word), do: \"#{n} #{word}s\"\n1069\t\n1070\t  defp tooltip_cta(\"nebu\"), do: \"Click to clear all filters\"\n1071\t  defp tooltip_cta(_kind), do: \"Click to filter the stream\"\n1072\t\n1073\t  defp badge_count(count) when count > 9, do: \"9+\"\n1074\t  defp badge_count(count), do: Integer.to_string(count)\n1075\t\n1076\t  defp badge_transform(radius) do\n1077\t    offset = coord(radius * 0.72)\n1078\t    \"translate(#{offset} -#{offset})\"\n1079\t  end\n1080\t\n1081\t  defp legend_rows do\n1082\t    [\n1083\t      {\"ok\", \"tm-legend-dot--ok\", \"Healthy — recedes into the background\"},\n1084\t      {\"warn\", \"tm-legend-dot--warn\", \"▲ Warnings in the last 60 s\"},\n1085\t      {\"error\", \"tm-legend-dot--error\", \"✕ Errors in the last 60 s — pulses\"},\n1086\t      {\"selected\", \"tm-legend-dot--selected\", \"Selected as stream filter\"},\n1087\t      {\"off\", \"tm-legend-dot--off\",\n1088\t       \"Long-polling endpoint — logging off by default; click to capture for 5 min\"},\n1089\t      {\"temp\", \"tm-legend-dot--temp\", \"Dashed = temporary capture, falls back to off automatically\"}\n1090\t    ]\n1091\t  end\n1092\t\n1093\t  defp level_pill_class(\"DEBUG\", true), do: \"text-text-secondary border-text-secondary\"\n1094\t  defp level_pill_class(\"INFO\", true), do: \"text-base-content border-text-secondary\"\n1095\t  defp level_pill_class(\"WARN\", true), do: \"text-warning border-warning bg-status-warn-bg\"\n1096\t  # UX gate V5: #ef4444 on status-error-bg is only 4.29:1 — the dummy's own\n1097\t  # pairing (#fca5a5, status-error-text token, 8.51:1) is used for text on\n1098\t  # that background.\n1099\t  defp level_pill_class(\"ERROR\", true), do: \"text-status-error-text border-error bg-status-error-bg\"\n1100\t  # UX gate V3: inactive pills are ENABLED toggle buttons — the disabled-text\n1101\t  # token (#4b5563, 2.35:1) fails WCAG 1.4.3 for them; text-secondary still\n1102\t  # reads clearly dimmed next to the colored active pills.\n1103\t  defp level_pill_class(_level, false), do: \"text-text-secondary border-base-300\"\n1104\t\n1105\t  defp level_text_class(\"ERROR\"), do: \"text-error\"\n1106\t  defp level_text_class(\"WARN\"), do: \"text-warning\"\n1107\t  defp level_text_class(\"DEBUG\"), do: \"text-text-secondary\"\n1108\t  defp level_text_class(_), do: \"text-text-secondary\"\n1109\t\n1110\t  defp status_pill_class(\"ok\"), do: \"bg-status-ok-bg text-success\"\n1111\t  defp status_pill_class(\"warn\"), do: \"bg-status-warn-bg text-warning\"\n1112\t  defp status_pill_class(\"error\"), do: \"bg-status-error-bg text-status-error-text\"\n1113\t\n1114\t  defp status_pill_text(\"ok\"), do: \"● Healthy\"\n1115\t  defp status_pill_text(\"warn\"), do: \"▲ Warnings\"\n1116\t  defp status_pill_text(\"error\"), do: \"✕ Errors\"\n1117\t\n1118\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1119\t  # War-room banner helpers (AC6)\n1120\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n1121\t\n1122\t  defp war_room_active?({:ok, _session}), do: true\n1123\t  defp war_room_active?(_), do: false\n1124\t\n1125\t  # Only ever called inside `:if={war_room_active?(@war_room)}` blocks, so a\n1126\t  # fallback clause would be dead code (flagged by --warnings-as-errors).\n1127\t  defp
