all-active\" do\n    test \"DEBUG/INFO/WARN/ERROR are all aria-pressed=true on mount\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert pill, \"expected a level pill for #{level}\"\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      end\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC9: leitstand shortcut — click one while all active isolates it; click it again restores all\" do\n    test \"clicking INFO while all 4 are active leaves ONLY INFO active\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      assert attr(find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"])), \"aria-pressed\") ==\n               \"true\"\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking the sole active level again restores all 4 to active\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      for level <- [\"DEBUG\", \"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\"\n      end\n    end\n\n    test \"clicking a level when SEVERAL (not all) are active is a plain toggle, not the shortcut\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      # Isolate INFO (all -> one), then toggle WARN back on: now {INFO, WARN}\n      # are active, i.e. \"several but not all\" — the next click must be a\n      # plain toggle, not re-trigger the shortcut.\n      view |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"INFO\"])) |> render_click()\n\n      view\n      |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"WARN\"]))\n      |> render_click()\n\n      html =\n        view\n        |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"ERROR\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      for level <- [\"INFO\", \"WARN\", \"ERROR\"] do\n        pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"#{level}\"]))\n        assert attr(pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"true\", \"expected #{level} active\"\n      end\n\n      debug_pill = find(html, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-level-pill\"][data-level=\"DEBUG\"]))\n      assert attr(debug_pill, \"aria-pressed\") == \"false\"\n    end\n       |> element(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-node\"][data-node-key=\"/admin/users\"]))\n        |> render_click()\n        |> parsed()\n\n      texts = html |> find_all(~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-stream-line\"])) |> Enum.map(&Floki.text/1)\n\n      assert Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-users-line\"))\n      refute Enum.any?(texts, &(&1 =~ \"distinctive-rooms-line\"))\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC9: the resizer's static, accessible markup contract\" do\n    test \"role=separator, aria-orientation=horizontal, tabindex=0, has a label naming the resize action\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      resizer = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-resizer\"]))\n\n      assert attr(resizer, \"role\") == \"separator\"\n      assert attr(resizer, \"aria-orientation\") == \"horizontal\"\n      assert attr(resizer, \"tabindex\") == \"0\"\n\n      # Test-review MINOR-5: the e2e suite locates the resizer via\n      # getByRole('separator', { name: /resize/i }) — the server-side test\n      # must pin the SAME accessible-name contract, or the two suites could\n      # green/red independently on the same markup.\n      label = attr(resizer, \"aria-label\")\n      assert label not in [nil, \"\"]\n      assert label =~ ~r/resize/i,\n             \"the resizer's accessible name must mention \\\"resize\\\" (e2e locator contract)\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n  # AC10: Accessibility — blocking requirements\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  describe \"AC10: every node is a focusable, labeled, toggle-state-exposing element\" do\n    test \"a satellite node's aria-label mentions its route, status, and counts\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"error\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()","numLines":70,"startLine":963,"totalLines":1150}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f38ee86e-aa47-49b9-b0c4-0e49340221ce","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

 describe \"AC6: war-room banner — hidden by default (no active session, no Nebu.Repo)\" do\n    # UX gate V1 (dev cycle 2): the original assertions pinned\n    # role=\"status\"/aria-live=\"polite\" ON the banner — an aria-live misuse,\n    # because the banner contains the per-second-ticking elapsed clock\n    # (role=\"status\" is implicitly atomic, so a screen reader would\n    # re-announce the whole banner sentence EVERY second for the entire\n    # session). The banner is now deliberately non-live; session start/stop\n    # transitions are announced as one-shot sentences through the page's\n    # sr-only announcer (covered by the AC10 announcer tests below).\n    test \"the banner element exists but is hidden, and is deliberately NOT a live region\" do\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      banner = find(parsed(html), ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]))\n\n      assert banner, \"AC6: the banner must always be present in markup (toggled via `hidden`)\"\n      assert attr(banner, \"hidden\") == \"\",\n             \"AC6: with no active session, the banner must carry the boolean `hidden` attribute\"\n\n      refute attr(banner, \"aria-live\"),\n             \"UX gate V1: the banner must NOT be a live region — it contains a \" <>\n               \"per-second ticking clock (state changes are announced via the announcer)\"\n\n      refute attr(banner, \"role\") == \"status\",\n             \"UX gate V1: role=status is an implicit atomic live region — see above\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  describe \"AC6: war-room banner — active session (requires a real session + Nebu.Repo)\" do\n    @describetag :integration\n\n    setup do\n      Nebu.Release.migrate()\n      :ok\n    end\n\n    test \"an active war-room session renders the banner with recording dot, user, and an Open War Room link\" do\n      {:ok, session} =\n        Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.start_session(%{\n          started_by: \"@alice:nebu.example\",\n          reason: \"trace-map-leitstand banner ATDD probe\",\n          max_entries: 100,\n          max_duration_s: 300\n        })\n\n      on_exit(fn -> Nebu.Admin.WarRoomManager.stop_session(session.id) end)\n\n      {:ok, _view, html} = mount_trace_map()\n      doc = parsed(html)\n      banner = find(doc, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-banner\"]))\n\n      refute attr(banner, \"hidden\"), \"AC6: an active session must NOT carry `hidden`\"\n      assert find(banner, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-dot\"]))\n      assert Floki.text(banner) =~ \"@alice:nebu.example\"\n\n      open_link = find(banner, ~s([data-testid=\"trace-map-war-room-open-link\"]))\n      assert attr(open_link, \"href\") == \"/admin/logs/war-room\"\n    end\n  end\n\n  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════","numLines":60,"startLine":716,"totalLines":1150}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"684ac8d0-4d51-4a17-99f8-fc2e39e2da78","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"1b71ce4b-a91c-4c53-886a-890afe2f61e8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01SiiJBi3BXMj2boeCpWuiwi","type":"tool_result","content":"360\t    # SVG — without this, the geometry module could compute correct numbers\n361\t    # the template never uses. All 4 canonical domains are observed in spec\n362\t    # order (first-observed order == spec order), so /_matrix/client must\n363\t    # sit at the spec's FIXED angle -48° on the R1=195 ring (spec §2). The\n364\t    # node's <g> is expected to position via `transform=\"translate(x y)\"`\n365\t    # (the dummy's own convention); coordinates are matched with a 0.6\n366\t    # tolerance so integer/decimal rounding in the template is acceptable.\n367\t    test \"a domain <g>'s rendered position matches the spec geometry (polar -48°, R1=195 for /_matrix/client)\" do\n368\t      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n369\t\n370\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"info\")\n371\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\", level: \"info\")\n372\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n373\t      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/health\", level: \"info\")\n374\t\n375\t      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n376\t      node = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\")\n377\t      assert node, \"expected a domain node for /_matrix/client\"\n378\t\n379\t      transform = attr(node, \"transform\")\n380\t\n381\t      assert transform,\n382\t             \"AC2: expected the domain <g> to carry a transform=\\\"translate(x y)\\\" \" <>\n383\t               \"with server-side computed coordinates\"\n384\t\n385\t      assert [_, x_str, y_str] =\n386\t               Regex.run(\n387\t                 ~r/translate\\(\\s*(-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)[\\s,]+(-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*\\)/,\n388\t                 transform\n389\t               ),\n390\t             \"AC2: transform #{inspect(transform)} did not parse as translate(x y)\"\n391\t\n392\t      {x, _} = Float.parse(x_str)\n393\t      {y, _} = Float.parse(y_str)\n394\t\n395\t      radians = -48.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0\n396\t      assert_in_delta x, 195.0 * :math.cos(radians), 0.6\n397\t      assert_in_delta y, 195.0 * :math.sin(radians), 0.6\n398\t    end\n399\t  end"}]},"uuid":"c6db1e0d-dd42-43d5-a250-8e467c58a5dd","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:39:05.957Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/test/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_leitstand_test.exs","content":"    # SVG — without this, the geometry module could compute correct numbers\n    # the template never uses. All 4 canonical domains are observed in spec\n    # order (first-observed order == spec order), so /_matrix/client must\n    # sit at the spec's FIXED angle -48° on the R1=195 ring (spec §2). The\n    # node's <g> is expected to position via `transform=\"translate(x y)\"`\n    # (the dummy's own convention); coordinates are matched with a 0.6\n    # tolerance so integer/decimal rounding in the template is acceptable.\n    test \"a domain <g>'s rendered position matches the spec geometry (polar -48°, R1=195 for /_matrix/client)\" do\n      {:ok, view, _html} = mount_trace_map()\n\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/client/v3/sync\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/_matrix/media/v3/upload\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/admin/users\", level: \"info\")\n      broadcast_entry(route_template: \"/health\", level: \"info\")\n\n      doc = render(view) |> parsed()\n      node = node_locator(doc, \"/_matrix/client\")\n      assert node, \"expected a domain node for /_matrix/client\"\n\n      transform = attr(node, \"transform\")\n\n      assert transform,\n             \"AC2: expected the domain <g> to carry a transform=\\\"translate(x y)\\\" \" <>\n               \"with server-side computed coordinates\"\n\n      assert [_, x_str, y_str] =\n               Regex.run(\n                 ~r/translate\\(\\s*(-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)[\\s,]+(-?\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)\\s*\\)/,\n                 transform\n               ),\n             \"AC2: transform #{inspect(transform)} did not parse as translate(x y)\"\n\n      {x, _} = Float.parse(x_str)\n      {y, _} = Float.parse(y_str)\n\n      radians = -48.0 * :math.pi() / 180.0\n      assert_in_delta x, 195.0 * :math.cos(radians), 0.6\n      assert_in_delta y, 195.0 * :math.sin(radians), 0.6\n    end\n  end","numLines":40,"startLine":360,"totalLines":1150}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"1b71ce4b-a91c-4c53-886a-890afe2f61e8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"e09c658b-dc52-4123-a199-56b1cdbab22b","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_011hemXANUrAPAP4Ef8y2z1Z","type":"tool_result","content":"server/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex | 14 ++++++++++----\n server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex  | 22 ++++++++++++++--------\n 2 files changed, 24 insertions(+), 12 deletions(-)\n\n--- Changes ---\n\nserver/lib/nebu/admin/layouts.ex\n  @@ -44,14 +44,20 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.Layouts do\n  -  # `/admin/logs/trace-map` and `/admin/logs/war-room` are stub routes for now\n  -  # (they render the existing logs page); the real pages land in the\n  -  # `trace-map-leitstand` / `war-room-dedicated-page` stories.\n  +  # `/admin/logs/trace-map` is the real Leitstand page since\n  +  # `trace-map-leitstand`; `/admin/logs/war-room` remains a stub route (it\n  +  # renders the existing logs page) until `war-room-dedicated-page` lands.\n  +  #\n  +  # trace-map-leitstand AC14 (UX gate advisory A1, decided by Phil\n  +  # 2026-07-07): the logs-config child is labeled \"Log Retention\" — NOT the\n  +  # bare \"Configuration\", which duplicated the top-level Configuration\n  +  # section's accessible name. The navkey `logs-config` and the\n  +  # /admin/logs/config route are unchanged; only the label.\n     @logs_nav_children [\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs\", key: \"logs-monitoring\", label: \"Live Stream\"},\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", key: \"logs-trace-map\", label: \"Trace Map\"},\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/war-room\", key: \"logs-war-room\", label: \"War Room\"},\n  -    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Configuration\"}\n  +    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Log Retention\"}\n     ]\n   \n     # NOTE on ordering: the Leitstand spec (§1) lists \"General\" first, but the\n  +10 -4\n\nserver/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex\n  @@ -216,15 +216,21 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.Router do\n  -      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream +\n  -      # Trace Map, subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic.\n  +      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream,\n  +      # subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic. The embedded\n  +      # Trace Map's Endpoint dimension was retired from this page by\n  +      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC13) in favor of the dedicated\n  +      # radial-map page below.\n         live \"/logs\", LogsLive, :index\n  -      # enhanced-logging/admin-sidebar-nesting (AC7 stub guidance): the nested\n  -      # \"Logs\" nav section links to these two pages before they exist as their\n  -      # own views. Both render the existing logs page so clicking the nav\n  -      # items never errors; the real pages land in the `trace-map-leitstand`\n  -      # and `war-room-dedicated-page` stories, which will repoint these routes.\n  -      live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive, :index\n  +      # enhanced-logging/trace-map-leitstand (AC1): the dedicated Trace Map\n  +      # \"Leitstand\" page — a SEPARATE LiveView module (it defines\n  +      # handle_params/3 for URL-driven filter state, which the shared\n  +      # LogsLive must never export; The navkey `logs-config` and the\n  +  # /admin/logs/config route are unchanged; only the label.\n     @logs_nav_children [\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs\", key: \"logs-monitoring\", label: \"Live Stream\"},\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/trace-map\", key: \"logs-trace-map\", label: \"Trace Map\"},\n       %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/war-room\", key: \"logs-war-room\", label: \"War Room\"},\n  -    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Configuration\"}\n  +    %{navigate: \"/admin/logs/config\", key: \"logs-config\", label: \"Log Retention\"}\n     ]\n   \n     # NOTE on ordering: the Leitstand spec (§1) lists \"General\" first, but the\n  +10 -4\n\nserver/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex\n  @@ -216,15 +216,21 @@ defmodule Nebu.Admin.Router do\n  -      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream +\n  -      # Trace Map, subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic.\n  +      # enhanced-logging/admin-logs-monitoring-view: live request-stream,\n  +      # subscribed to the operational-log-store's PubSub topic. {"parentUuid":"56d16397-3624-4989-9d31-72e8c41e65c8","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cco7CFK7NbAeLE232WvXk","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01H9zQgAQuZhi6RU2StF6oUd","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex","offset":370,"limit":20},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2950,"cache_read_input_tokens":101384,"output_tokens":580,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2950},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":580,"cache_read_input_tokens":101384,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2950,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2950,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cco7CEEctKnfKC5gWVgcp","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"a3b51bd1-6185-489f-9bfe-b2887547e5a0","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:40:43.840Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
{"parentUuid":"a3b51bd1-6185-489f-9bfe-b2887547e5a0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"2cbe9dab-e0ba-479d-9c34-0deb3a6402aa","agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01H9zQgAQuZhi6RU2StF6oUd","type":"tool_result","content":"370\t  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n371\t\n372\t  # Bug fix (trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix, AC1/AC2/AC5): fixes the root\n373\t  # cause directly — `@active_filters` becomes real, persistent\n374\t  # `socket.assigns` state instead of the original, permanently-`false`\n375\t  # `active:` value every Trace Map bubble used to render with. Any LATER,\n376\t  # unrelated re-render (e.g. `handle_info/2` ingesting a new log entry,\n377\t  # which changes `@level_counts`/`@source_counts`/`@endpoint_counts`) now\n378\t  # reads THIS real state for the `active:` attr instead of a hardcoded\n379\t  # constant, so it can no longer silently overwrite an operator's click.\n380\t  #\n381\t  # Pushed via `JS.push/2` (see `bubble_js/3` below), NOT `JS.push` blocking\n382\t  # the click — `JS.dispatch(\"nebu:trace-bubble-click\")` (UNCHANGED from the\n383\t  # original admin-logs-monitoring-view story) is always the FIRST command\n384\t  # in the list and fires synchronously/instantly in the browser; this\n385\t  # `handle_event` round-trip happens asynchronously alongside it, so AC2's\n386\t  # \"no perceptible click lag\" holds regardless of server latency.\n387\t  #\n388\t  # dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M1: each dimension holds a\n389\t  # LIST of active values, not a single scalar — toggling ADDS the value if"}]},"uuid":"9e3b47cc-cee5-4b4e-abcc-da000d934025","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:40:43.856Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex","content":"  # ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════\n\n  # Bug fix (trace-map-filter-reset-bugfix, AC1/AC2/AC5): fixes the root\n  # cause directly — `@active_filters` becomes real, persistent\n  # `socket.assigns` state instead of the original, permanently-`false`\n  # `active:` value every Trace Map bubble used to render with. Any LATER,\n  # unrelated re-render (e.g. `handle_info/2` ingesting a new log entry,\n  # which changes `@level_counts`/`@source_counts`/`@endpoint_counts`) now\n  # reads THIS real state for the `active:` attr instead of a hardcoded\n  # constant, so it can no longer silently overwrite an operator's click.\n  #\n  # Pushed via `JS.push/2` (see `bubble_js/3` below), NOT `JS.push` blocking\n  # the click — `JS.dispatch(\"nebu:trace-bubble-click\")` (UNCHANGED from the\n  # original admin-logs-monitoring-view story) is always the FIRST command\n  # in the list and fires synchronously/instantly in the browser; this\n  # `handle_event` round-trip happens asynchronously alongside it, so AC2's\n  # \"no perceptible click lag\" holds regardless of server latency.\n  #\n  # dev-story cycle 1, code-review MAJOR finding M1: each dimension holds a\n  # LIST of active values, not a single scalar — toggling ADDS the value if","numLines":20,"startLine":370,"totalLines":1313}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a3b51bd1-6185-489f-9bfe-b2887547e5a0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}

{"parentUuid":"416c08f7-d35c-4bbc-bd95-0644acdbd7de","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"aa6ecfebdffac351c","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011Cco7HhXLcob8zuis9vhc1","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Review complete. Full report below.\n\n---\n\n## Code Review — `trace-map-leitstand` (enhanced-logging), cycle 3\n\n**Verdict: 1 MAJOR, 2 MINOR, 5 INFO**\n\n---\n\n### MAJOR\n\n**M1 — Double-counted events for any route that is its own domain (`/health` guaranteed)**\n`server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex:319-360` (`ingest_entry` → `record_event`).\n`node_keys = [route, TraceMapTree.domain_for_endpoint(route), \"nebu\"]` is fed to `record_event`'s `Enum.reduce` with **no `Enum.uniq`**. For any single-segment route, `domain_for_endpoint(route) == route` (verified: `domain_for_endpoint(\"/health\") == \"/health\"`, `trace_map_tree.ex:272-278`), so the list is `[\"/health\", \"/health\", \"nebu\"]` and the event is recorded **twice** into the `/health` node state. Every derived count is therefore 2× actual: the ▲/✕ badge count, tooltip (`counts_text`), inspector stats (`Requests/Warnings/Errors / 60 s`), and the node `aria-label`. This violates the count-accuracy requirements of AC3, AC7, AC8, and AC10 on a node the spec names explicitly (`/health` is one of the 4 canonical domains; a health endpoint is always logged as exactly `/health`). Also affects any bare `/admin`, `/metrics`, etc. It is **not** status-affecting (the `> 0` threshold is unchanged), which is why it slips past the status tests — and **no test asserts `/health`'s counts** (`trace_map_leitstand_test.exs:373` uses `/health` only for geometry positioning).\nThis is the **same `/health` dual domain/satellite root cause** the dev fixed in cycle 2 for DOM ids (kind-prefixing) — it re-surfaced in a second code path. Fix: `Enum.uniq(node_keys)` before recording (and add a `/health` count regression test).\n\n---\n\n### MINOR\n\n**m1 — `normalize_selected/1` does not de-duplicate URL selections**\n`trace_map.ex:185-192`. A bookmarked/crafted URL with `endpoint[]=X&endpoint[]=X` seeds `@selected = [\"X\",\"X\"]` → two identical filter chips render, and `toggle_node` (`List.delete`, `:208`) removes only one, leaving a lingering chip and an inconsistent selection. Low blast radius but a real functional glitch. Add `Enum.uniq/1` in `normalize_selected/1`.\n\n**m2 — No regression test for the V1 war-room announcer transition path**\n`trace_map.ex:275-294` (`handle_info(:refresh_war_room_status)`) now emits the one-shot `\"War Room session started/ended\"` sentence into `@announcement` — this is the *replacement* for the removed banner-as-live-region behavior (UX gate V1). Only the **error** announcer is covered by a unit test (`trace_map_leitstand_test.exs:1073`); the war-room start/stop announcement has no assertion anywhere. Matches the known \"fix applied but lacks a direct regression test\" pattern. (Requires Repo + session + poll, so it needs an integration-tagged test.)\n\n---\n\n### INFO\n\n**i1 — Stale comment references removed assign.** `logs_live.ex:377` still cites `@endpoint_counts`, which the AC13 retirement removed (`@filter_dimensions` is now `[\"level\",\"source\"]`, `:99`). Comment-only; no code impact.\n\n**i2 — Per-entry full re-render cost.** `render/1` rebuilds the whole view-model on every PubSub entry *and* every 1 s tick: geometry `layout`, and `TraceMapStatus.counts/status` (each an `Enum.count`/`filter` over the node's full 60 s timestamp list) for every node, several times per render. Under high operational-log volume a single hot node's window can hold thousands of timestamps, making each render O(events-in-window) → effectively quadratic across a burst, per connected client. The log stream is a plain capped list re-rendered wholesale rather than Phoenix LiveView streams. This is the documented, accepted \"Option A\" tradeoff (admin-only, low concurrency, bounded node count) — flagging only because the per-render recount amplification exceeds what \"Option A / prune on a timer\" described; `Phoenix.LiveView` streams would be the idiomatic fix for the stream panel if volume ever becomes an issue.\n\n**i3 — Visual node duplication for singleton-domain routes.** `/health` renders as **both** a domain circle and a satellite circle (single satellite sits on the domain angle at R2). A design consequence of reusing the tree grouping; not clearly wrong per AC2 (\"satellites are observed route_templates\"), but redundant. Distinct from M1 (which is the count bug); worth a product glance.\n\n**i4 — AC1 literal deviation (documented).** AC1 prose specifies `server/lib/nebu/web/router.ex`, `get \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive, :trace_map`. Implementation uses `server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex`, `live \"/logs/trace-map\", LogsLive.TraceMap, :index` — consistent with where admin LiveViews actually live and justified in the Dev Agent Record (separate module for the `handle_params/3` isolation reason). Intent (dedicated route + `@active_nav=\"logs-trace-map\"`) is satisfied; the `:index` vs `:trace_map` action atom is cosmetic for a single-mount module.\n\n**i5 — Memory-bound edge.** `TraceMapStatus` prunes only on `record/3`; a node that bursts then goes silent retains that burst's timestamps until its next event (or process death). Bounded by burst size × (finite) node count and self-heals on next event — acceptable, noting the moduledoc's \"bounded by 60 s of traffic\" is slightly optimistic for silent nodes.\n\n---\n\n### Verified clean (checked, no finding)\n\n- **Dev-modified staged tests — all 3 corrections are justified, NOT assertion-weakening:** (a) `viewBox`→`viewbox` lookup (`:330`) is a correct Floki/lazy_html lowercasing fact; the template still renders correct camelCase `viewBox`. (b) WARN→INFO shortcut-isolate rewrite (`:965-982`) keeps the identical assertions (info line kept, warn line hidden) and the original clicked-WARN-while-all-active step provably asserted the opposite of the AC9 shortcut outcome. (c) Banner-non-live rewrite (`:726-740`) now *positively* asserts the banner is not a live region per UX-gate V1 — a stronger, correct a11y assertion, with the announcement path covered separately.\n- **Public-API (ADR-020):** `Nebu.Admin.*` → `Nebu.Web.LogStore.get_logs/1` mirrors the existing `Nebu.Admin.LogsLive` usage exactly (`logs_live.ex:311/333`); `Nebu.Web` is sanctioned shared infra. No new cross-domain violation.\n- **Cluster-safety (ADR-019):** all state in socket assigns; `Process.send_after` is node-local; PubSub is cluster-wide; no ETS/global/`:persistent_term` state.\n- **CSP / XSS:** tooltip and elapsed-clock built via `textContent` + `getAttribute` (`app.js:1409-1463`), no `innerHTML`; all node/chip/announcer text is HEEx-escaped; single `TraceMapLeitstand` hook, no inline JS or `on*=`.\n- **URL round-trip:** bracket-array encode via `URI.encode_www_form` + `patch_path` (`:245-250`); `normalize_selected` rejects non-binary/empty; malformed PubSub entries fall through `handle_info(_other, ...)` (`:302`).\n- **Status window math:** strict `now_ms - ts < 60_000` boundary matches AC3's `> now()-60s`; auto-heal falls out of pure recompute + 1 s tick; worst-child propagation includes parent-own events defensively.\n- **Geometry:** constants and fallback match spec §2 and are covered by `trace_map_geometry_test.exs`.\n\n**Files:** `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map.ex` (M1, m1, m2, i2, i3), `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live/trace_map_status.ex` (i5), `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/logs_live.ex` (i1), `/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/server/lib/nebu/admin/router.ex` (i4).\n\nMemory updated: recorded the recurring `/health` dual domain/satellite key collision as `feedback_dual_domain_satellite_key.md` (now bitten 3 code paths in this epic)."}],"stop_reason":"end_turn","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":251,"cache_read_input_tokens":109743,"output_tokens":3194,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":251},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":3194,"cache_read_input_tokens":109743,"cache_creation_input_tokens":251,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":251,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011Cco7HfYGpStypC3CR2qwa","attributionAgent":"nebu-code-reviewer","type":"assistant","uuid":"a08d7308-53a4-4f81-8ec3-1597d09a88d9","timestamp":"2026-07-07T17:42:33.478Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/philippbeyerlein/dev/01_gitlab/1200_philippb/open-chat/scripts","sessionId":"84ea81ea-bcb7-4a61-b7fb-16f76ec74d82","version":"2.1.199","gitBranch":"feature/enhanced-logging"}
